config folder have:
database.js
// is emplty


db.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
require('dotenv').config();

const connectDB = async () => {
    console.log("Mongo URI:", process.env.MONGO_URI);

    try {
        await mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGO_URI, {
            useNewUrlParser: true,
            useUnifiedTopology: true,
        });
        console.log('MongoDB Connected');
    } catch (err) {
        console.error(err.message);
        process.exit(1);
    }
};

module.exports = connectDB;




controllers folder:
orderController.js
const Order = require('../models/Order');

exports.createOrder = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const cart = req.session.cart;

        if (!cart || cart.length === 0) {
            return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: "Your cart is empty." });
        }

        const { shippingAddress, paymentMethod } = req.body;

        // Calculate Totals
        const subtotal = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
        const tax = subtotal * 0.08;
        const total = subtotal + tax;

        // Generate Order Number manually here to ensure it exists before validation
        const date = new Date();
        const year = date.getFullYear().toString().slice(-2);
        const month = (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const day = date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const random = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
        const generatedOrderNumber = `ORD-${year}${month}${day}-${random}`;

        // Create Order Object
        const orderData = {
            orderNumber: generatedOrderNumber,
            items: cart.map(item => ({
                product: item.productId,
                name: item.name,
                price: item.price,
                quantity: item.quantity,
                subtotal: item.price * item.quantity
            })),
            subtotal,
            tax,
            total,
            status: 'pending',
            shippingAddress,
            paymentMethod,
            paymentStatus: 'pending'
        };

        // ONLY add user ID if the user is actually logged in
        if (req.session.userId) {
            orderData.user = req.session.userId;
        }

        const newOrder = new Order(orderData);
        await newOrder.save();

        // Clear the cart
        req.session.cart = [];

        res.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Order placed!",
            orderNumber: newOrder.orderNumber
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Order Error:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
    }
};



productController.js
const Product = require('../models/Product');

// Get all products
exports.getAllProducts = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const products = await Product.find().populate('category');
        res.json(products);
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
    }
};

// Get single product by ID
exports.getProductById = async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const product = await Product.findById(req.params.id).populate('category');
        if (!product) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Product not found' });
        res.json(product);
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: error.message });
    }
};




userControllers.js
exports.getProfile = async (req, res) => {
  try {
    res.json(req.user); // ✅ logged-in user ONLY
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ message: "Server error" });
  }
};





middlewares folder have:
auth.js
const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");
const User = require("../models/User");

module.exports = async (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(" ")[1];
    if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ message: "Unauthorized" });

    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
    req.user = await User.findById(decoded.id).select("-password");

    next();
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(401).json({ message: "Invalid token" });
  }
};

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const auditLogSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    action: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        enum: ['create', 'update', 'delete', 'login', 'logout', 'purchase', 'refund']
    },
    entity: {
        type: String,
        required: true
    },
    entityId: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
    user: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'User'
    },
    ipAddress: String,
    userAgent: String,
    oldValues: mongoose.Schema.Types.Mixed,
    newValues: mongoose.Schema.Types.Mixed,
    changes: mongoose.Schema.Types.Mixed,
    description: String,
    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('AuditLog', auditLogSchema);

Cart.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const cartSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    user: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'User',
        required: true
    },
    sessionId: String, // For guest users
    items: [{
        product: {
            type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: 'Product',
            required: true
        },
        quantity: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 1,
            default: 1
        },
        price: Number, // Price at time of adding to cart
        addedAt: {
            type: Date,
            default: Date.now
        }
    }],
    couponCode: String,
    discountAmount: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    notes: String,
    lastUpdated: {
        type: Date,
        default: Date.now
    },
    expiresAt: {
        type: Date,
        default: () => new Date(+new Date() + 7*24*60*60*1000) // 7 days
    }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Cart', cartSchema);

Coupon.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const couponSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    code: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        unique: true,
        uppercase: true
    },
    name: String,
    description: String,
    discountType: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['percentage', 'fixed', 'free_shipping'],
        required: true
    },
    discountValue: {
        type: Number,
        required: true
    },
    minimumPurchase: Number,
    maximumDiscount: Number,
    validFrom: {
        type: Date,
        required: true
    },
    validUntil: {
        type: Date,
        required: true
    },
    usageLimit: Number,
    usedCount: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    userLimit: Number, // Max uses per user
    applicableProducts: [{
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Product'
    }],
    applicableCategories: [{
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Category'
    }],
    excludedProducts: [{
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Product'
    }],
    isActive: {
        type: Boolean,
        default: true
    },
    createdBy: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Employee'
    },
    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    },
    updatedAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Coupon', couponSchema);

Inventory.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const inventorySchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    product: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Product',
        required: true
    },
    sku: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        unique: true
    },
    quantity: {
        type: Number,
        required: true,
        default: 0
    },
    reserved: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    available: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    lowStockThreshold: {
        type: Number,
        default: 10
    },
    location: {
        warehouse: String,
        aisle: String,
        shelf: String,
        bin: String
    },
    lastRestocked: Date,
    nextRestockDate: Date,
    supplier: {
        name: String,
        contact: String,
        leadTime: Number // in days
    },
    cost: Number,
    reorderQuantity: Number,
    status: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['in_stock', 'low_stock', 'out_of_stock', 'discontinued'],
        default: 'in_stock'
    },
    notes: String,
    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    },
    updatedAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

// Calculate available quantity before saving
inventorySchema.pre('save', function(next) {
    this.available = this.quantity - this.reserved;
    next();
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Inventory', inventorySchema);

Order.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const orderSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    orderNumber: {
        type: String,
        unique: true
    },
    user: { 
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, 
        ref: 'User', 
        required: false 
    },
    items: [{
        product: { 
            type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, 
            ref: 'Product' 
        },
        name: String,
        sku: String,
        price: Number,
        quantity: Number,
        image: String,
        subtotal: Number
    }],
    subtotal: { 
        type: Number, 
        required: true 
    },
    tax: { 
        type: Number, 
        default: 0 
    },
    shippingCost: { 
        type: Number, 
        default: 0 
    },
    discount: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    total: { 
        type: Number, 
        required: true 
    },
    status: { 
        type: String, 
        enum: [
            'pending', 
            'confirmed', 
            'processing', 
            'shipped', 
            'delivered', 
            'cancelled', 
            'refunded'
        ],
        default: 'pending'
    },
    shippingAddress: {
        recipientName: String,
        street: String,
        city: String,
        state: String,
        zipCode: String,
        country: String,
        phone: String
    },
    billingAddress: {
        sameAsShipping: { type: Boolean, default: true },
        recipientName: String,
        street: String,
        city: String,
        state: String,
        zipCode: String,
        country: String
    },
    paymentMethod: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['credit_card', 'paypal', 'apple_pay', 'google_pay', 'bank_transfer'],
        required: true
    },
    paymentStatus: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['pending', 'paid', 'failed', 'refunded'],
        default: 'pending'
    },
    trackingNumber: String,
    shippingCarrier: String,
    estimatedDelivery: Date,
    actualDelivery: Date,
    notes: String,
    customerNotes: String,
    couponCode: String,
    salesRep: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Employee'
    },
    ipAddress: String,
    userAgent: String,
    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    },
    updatedAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

// Generate order number before saving
orderSchema.pre('save', async function(next) {
    if (!this.orderNumber) {
        const date = new Date();
        const year = date.getFullYear().toString().slice(-2);
        const month = (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const day = date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0');
        const random = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
        this.orderNumber = `ORD-${year}${month}${day}-${random}`;
    }
    next();
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Order', orderSchema);

Payment.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const paymentSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    order: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Order',
        required: true
    },
    user: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'User',
        required: true
    },
    paymentId: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        unique: true
    },
    amount: {
        type: Number,
        required: true
    },
    currency: {
        type: String,
        default: 'USD'
    },
    method: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['credit_card', 'paypal', 'apple_pay', 'google_pay', 'bank_transfer'],
        required: true
    },
    status: {
        type: String,
        enum: ['pending', 'completed', 'failed', 'refunded', 'cancelled'],
        default: 'pending'
    },
    paymentDetails: {
        // For credit cards
        cardLastFour: String,
        cardBrand: String,
        cardExpiry: String,
        
        // For PayPal
        paypalEmail: String,
        paypalTransactionId: String,
        
        // For bank transfer
        bankName: String,
        accountLastFour: String
    },
    receiptUrl: String,
    refundedAmount: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    refundReason: String,
    processedBy: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Employee'
    },
    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    },
    updatedAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

// Generate payment ID before saving
paymentSchema.pre('save', async function(next) {
    if (!this.paymentId) {
        const timestamp = Date.now();
        const random = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000);
        this.paymentId = `PAY-${timestamp}-${random}`;
    }
    next();
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Payment', paymentSchema);

const mongoose = require('mongoose');

const productSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    name: { type: String, required: true },
    description: String,
    price: { type: Number, required: true },
    quantity: { type: Number, default: 10 },
    category: {
        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Category'
    },
    image: String,
    isFeatured: { type: Boolean, default: false },
    createdAt: { type: Date, default: Date.now }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model('Product', productSchema);

        type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        ref: 'Employee'

    createdAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    },
    updatedAt: { 
        type: Date, 
        default: Date.now 
    }
});

// Ensure one review per user per product
reviewSchema.index({ product: 1, user: 1 }, { unique: true });

module.exports = mongoose.model('Review', reviewSchema);

check-missing.html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Check Missing Pages</title>
    <style>
        body { font-family: Arial; padding: 20px; }
        .status { padding: 10px; margin: 5px; border-radius: 5px; }
        .exists { background: #d4edda; color: #155724; }
        .missing { background: #f8d7da; color: #721c24; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Checking HTML Files</h1>
    <div id="results"></div>
    
    <script>
        const pages = [
            'index.html', 'products.html', 'cart.html', 'product-detail.html',
            'about.html', 'blog.html', 'checkout.html', 'collections.html',
            'login.html', 'register.html', 'profile.html', 'search.html',
            'settings.html', '404.html'
        ];
        
        async function checkPage(page) {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(page);
                if (response.status === 200) {
                    return `<div class="status exists">✅ ${page} - EXISTS</div>`;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                return `<div class="status missing">❌ ${page} - MISSING or ERROR</div>`;
            }
        }
        
        async function checkAll() {
            const resultsDiv = document.getElementById('results');
            for (const page of pages) {
                const result = await checkPage(page);
                resultsDiv.innerHTML += result;
            }
        }
        
        checkAll();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        function selectPayment(method) {
            // Remove selected class from all
            document.querySelectorAll('.payment-method').forEach(el => {
                el.classList.remove('selected');
            });

            // Add selected class to clicked
            event.currentTarget.classList.add('selected');

            // Show appropriate form
            document.getElementById('creditCardForm').style.display = method === 'credit' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('paypalForm').style.display = method === 'paypal' ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        async function processCheckout() {
            // 1. Collect Form Data
            const shippingData = {
                recipientName: document.getElementById('fullName').value,
                street: document.getElementById('address').value,
                city: document.getElementById('city').value,
                state: document.getElementById('state').value,
                zipCode: document.getElementById('zipCode').value,
                country: document.getElementById('country').value,
                phone: document.getElementById('phone').value
            };

            // Simple validation
            if (!shippingData.recipientName || !shippingData.street) {
                alert("Please fill in your shipping details.");
                return;
            }

            const paymentMethod = document.querySelector('.payment-method.selected div').innerText.toLowerCase().replace(' ', '_');

            // 2. Disable button to prevent double clicking
            const btn = document.querySelector('.checkout-btn');
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';

            try {
                // 3. Send to Backend
                const response = await fetch('/api/orders', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        shippingAddress: shippingData,
                        paymentMethod: paymentMethod
                    })
                });

                const data = await response.json();

                if (data.success) {
                    alert('🎉 Order placed successfully! Order Number: ' + data.orderNumber);
                    window.location.href = '/profile'; // Redirect to profile to see orders
                } else {
                    alert('❌ Order failed: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-lock"></i> Place Order';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Checkout error:', error);
                alert('⚠️ Network error. Please check your connection.');
                btn.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
                if (cartCount) {
                    const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
                    cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
            }
        }

        updateCartCount();
    </script>
</body>

</html>

    <script>
        document.getElementById('loginForm').addEventListener('submit', async function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const remember = document.getElementById('remember')?.checked;

            // Simple validation
            if (!email || !password) {
                alert('Please fill in all fields');
                return;
            }

            if (!email.includes('@')) {
                alert('Please enter a valid email address');
                return;
            }

            // Show loading state
            const submitBtn = this.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
            const originalText = submitBtn.innerHTML;
            submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Signing In...';
            submitBtn.disabled = true;

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
                });

                const data = await response.json();

                if (data.success) {
                    // Store user info in localStorage
                    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
                    localStorage.setItem('isLoggedIn', 'true');

                    // Show success message
                    alert('✅ Login successful! Redirecting to home page...');

                    // Redirect to home page
                    window.location.href = '/';

                } else {
                    // Show error message
                    alert('❌ ' + (data.error || 'Login failed'));
                    submitBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                    submitBtn.disabled = false;
                }

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Login error:', error);
                alert('⚠️ Network error. Please try again.');
                submitBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                submitBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        // Social login buttons
        document.querySelector('button[style*="border-color: #db4437"]')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
            alert('Google login would be implemented here');
        });

        document.querySelector('button[style*="border-color: #1877f2"]')?.addEventListener('click', function () {
            alert('Facebook login would be implemented here');
        });

        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
                if (cartCount) {
                    const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
                    cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
                    cartCount.style.display = totalItems > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
            }
        }

        updateCartCount();
    </script>


</body>

</html>

    <title>My Orders - Computer Store</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="css/style.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.4.0/css/all.min.css">
</head>
<body>
    <!-- Use the standard navbar from step 2 -->
    <nav class="navbar">
        <!-- Copy the navbar from step 2 here -->
    </nav>

    <div class="breadcrumb">
        <div class="container">
            <ol>
                <li><a href="/">Home</a></li>
                <li><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></li>
                <li><a href="/profile">Profile</a></li>
                <li><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></li>
                <li>My Orders</li>
            </ol>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="container" style="padding: 2rem 0;">
        <h1>My Orders</h1>
        <p style="color: var(--gray);">Your order history will appear here.</p>
        
        <div id="ordersList" style="margin-top: 2rem;">
            <!-- Orders will be loaded here -->
            <div class="loading-spinner">Loading orders...</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="js/app.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Simple order loading logic
        async function loadOrders() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/me');
                const userData = await response.json();
                
                if (userData.success) {
                    document.getElementById('ordersList').innerHTML = `
                        <div style="background: white; padding: 2rem; border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: var(--shadow); text-align: center;">
                            <i class="fas fa-box-open" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--gray-light); margin-bottom: 1rem;"></i>
                            <h3>No Orders Yet</h3>
                            <p style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">You haven't placed any orders yet.</p>
                            <a href="/products" class="btn btn-primary">
                                <i class="fas fa-shopping-bag"></i> Start Shopping
                            </a>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error loading orders:', error);
            }
        }
        
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            loadOrders();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
                if (cartCount) {
                    const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
                    cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
            }
        }

        updateCartCount();
    </script>

<html lang="en">

    <!-- In register.html -->
    <script>
        document.getElementById('registerForm').addEventListener('submit', async function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            const firstName = document.getElementById('firstName').value;
            const lastName = document.getElementById('lastName').value;
            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;
            const phone = document.getElementById('phone').value;
            const terms = document.getElementById('terms').checked;

            // Validation
            if (!firstName || !lastName || !email || !password || !confirmPassword) {
                alert('Please fill in all required fields');
                return;
            }

            if (!terms) {
                alert('You must agree to the Terms of Service');
                return;
            }

            if (password !== confirmPassword) {
                alert('Passwords do not match');
                return;
            }

            if (password.length < 6) {
                alert('Password must be at least 6 characters');
                return;
            }

            // Show loading state
            const submitBtn = this.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
            const originalText = submitBtn.innerHTML;
            submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Creating Account...';
            submitBtn.disabled = true;

            try {
                const userData = {
                    name: `${firstName} ${lastName}`,
                    email,
                    password,
                    phone: phone || '',
                    address: ''
                };

                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/register', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(userData)
                });

                const data = await response.json();

                if (data.success) {
                    alert('✅ Registration successful! Please login.');

                    // Auto redirect to login page
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = '/login';
                    }, 1500);

                } else {
                    alert('❌ ' + (data.error || 'Registration failed'));
                    submitBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                    submitBtn.disabled = false;
                }

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Registration error:', error);
                alert('⚠️ Network error. Please try again.');
                submitBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                submitBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
                if (cartCount) {
                    const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
                    cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
                    cartCount.style.display = totalItems > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
            }
        }

        updateCartCount();
    </script>



</body>

</html>

    <script>
        // Load search results
        async function loadSearchResults() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/products');
                const products = await response.json();
                
                const searchTerm = 'gaming'; // This would normally come from URL params
                const filteredProducts = products.filter(product => 
                    product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
                    product.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
                );
                
                const container = document.getElementById('searchResults');
                if (filteredProducts.length === 0) {
                    container.innerHTML = `
                        <div style="grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align: center; padding: 4rem;">
                            <i class="fas fa-search" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--gray-light); margin-bottom: 1rem;"></i>
                            <h3 style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 1rem;">No results found</h3>
                            <p style="color: var(--gray);">Try adjusting your search terms or filters</p>
                        </div>
                    `;
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = filteredProducts.map(product => `
                        <div class="card">
                            ${product.isFeatured ? '<span class="card-badge">Featured</span>' : ''}
                            <img src="${product.image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200?text=Computer'}" 
                                 alt="${product.name}" 
                                 class="card-image">
                            <div class="card-content">
                                <h3>${product.name}</h3>
                                <p>${product.description ? product.description.substring(0, 80) + '...' : ''}</p>
                                <div class="price">$${product.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                                <div class="stock-status">
                                    ${product.quantity > 0 ? 
                                        `<span class="in-stock"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> In Stock</span>` : 
                                        '<span class="out-of-stock"><i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> Out of Stock</span>'
                                    }
                                </div>
                                <div class="card-actions">
                                    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" onclick="addToCart('${product._id}')">
                                        <i class="fas fa-cart-plus"></i> Add to Cart
                                    </button>
                                    <a href="/product/${product._id}" class="btn btn-outline btn-block">
                                        <i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View Details
                                    </a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `).join('');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error loading search results:', error);
            }
        }
        
        // Add to cart function
        async function addToCart(productId) {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart/add', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        productId: productId,
                        quantity: 1
                    })
                });
                
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('Product added to cart!');
                    updateCartCount();
                } else {
                    alert('Failed to add to cart: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
                }
                
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error adding to cart:', error);
                alert('Network error. Please try again.');
            }
        }
        
        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
                if (cartCount) {
                    const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
                    cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
            }
        }
        
        // Initialize
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            loadSearchResults();
            updateCartCount();
            
            // Search form
            const searchForm = document.getElementById('searchForm');
            if (searchForm) {
                searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
                    if (searchInput && searchInput.value.trim()) {
                        // In real app, this would redirect to search with query params
                        alert('Searching for: ' + searchInput.value);
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        function exportData() {
            if (confirm('This will export all your account data. Continue?')) {

        function deleteAccount() {
            if (confirm('WARNING: This will permanently delete your account and all associated data. This action cannot be undone. Are you sure?')) {
                alert('Account deletion requested. A confirmation email has been sent.');
            }
        }
        
        // Load cart count
        async function updateCartCount() {

test-cart.html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Test Cart</title>
    <style>
        body { font-family: Arial; padding: 20px; }
        button { margin: 5px; padding: 10px; }
        .success { color: green; }
        .error { color: red; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Cart API Test</h1>
    
    <div>
        <h3>Test Product ID: <code id="productId">695a826985a127465d4fff96</code></h3>
        <button onclick="addToCart()">Add to Cart</button>
        <button onclick="getCart()">Get Cart</button>
        <button onclick="clearCart()">Clear Cart</button>
    </div>
    
    <div id="result" style="margin-top: 20px; padding: 10px; background: #f0f0f0;"></div>
    
    <script>
        const productId = '695a826985a127465d4fff96'; // Alienware
        
        async function addToCart() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart/add', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ productId, quantity: 1 })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('ADD TO CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        async function getCart() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('GET CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        async function clearCart() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart/clear', { method: 'DELETE' });
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('CLEAR CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        function showResult(title, data, isError = false) {
            const resultDiv = document.getElementById('result');
            resultDiv.innerHTML = `
                <h4 class="${isError ? 'error' : 'success'}">${title}</h4>
                <pre>${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}</pre>
            `;
        }
        
        // Test on load
        getCart();
    </script>
</body>
</html>





test-login.html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Test Login</title>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Test Login</h1>
    <form id="testLoginForm">
        <input type="email" id="testEmail" placeholder="Email" value="admin@computerstore.com"><br>
        <input type="password" id="testPassword" placeholder="Password" value="admin123"><br>
        <button type="submit">Test Login</button>
    </form>
    
    <div id="result"></div>
    
    <script>
        document.getElementById('testLoginForm').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            const email = document.getElementById('testEmail').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('testPassword').value;
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
                });
                
                const data = await response.json();
                document.getElementById('result').innerHTML = 
                    `<pre>${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}</pre>`;
                    
            } catch (error) {
                document.getElementById('result').innerHTML = 
                    `<pre style="color:red">Error: ${error.message}</pre>`;
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>



test.html
<!-- public/test.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Test Computer Store</title>
    <style>
        body { font-family: Arial; padding: 20px; }
        .success { color: green; }
        .error { color: red; }
        button { margin: 5px; padding: 10px; }
        .product { border: 1px solid #ccc; padding: 10px; margin: 10px; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Computer Store Test Page</h1>
    
    <div>
        <h3>Test Cart Functionality</h3>
        <button onclick="testAPI()">Test API</button>
        <button onclick="testProducts()">Test Products</button>
        <button onclick="testCart()">Test Cart</button>
        <button onclick="clearCart()">Clear Cart</button>
    </div>
    
    <div id="result" style="margin-top: 20px; padding: 10px; background: #f0f0f0;"></div>
    
    <div id="products" style="margin-top: 20px;"></div>
    
    <script>
        // Test basic API
        async function testAPI() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/test');
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('API TEST:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        // Test products
        async function testProducts() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/products');
                const products = await response.json();
                
                showResult('PRODUCTS:', `${products.length} products loaded`);
                
                // Display products
                const container = document.getElementById('products');
                container.innerHTML = '<h3>Available Products:</h3>' + 
                    products.map(p => `
                        <div class="product">
                            <strong>${p.name}</strong><br>
                            Price: $${p.price.toFixed(2)}<br>
                            Stock: ${p.quantity}<br>
                            <button onclick="addToCart('${p._id}')">Add to Cart</button>
                        </div>
                    `).join('');
                
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        // Test cart
        async function testCart() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        // Add to cart
        async function addToCart(productId) {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart/add', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ productId, quantity: 1 })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('ADD TO CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        // Clear cart
        async function clearCart() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/cart/clear', { method: 'DELETE' });
                const data = await response.json();
                showResult('CLEAR CART:', data);
            } catch (error) {
                showResult('ERROR:', error, true);
            }
        }
        
        function showResult(title, data, isError = false) {
            const resultDiv = document.getElementById('result');
            resultDiv.innerHTML = `
                <h4 class="${isError ? 'error' : 'success'}">${title}</h4>
                <pre>${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}</pre>
            `;
        }
        
        // Run tests on load
        window.onload = function() {
            testAPI();
            setTimeout(testProducts, 500);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

app.js
// app.js - Main application JavaScript
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('ComputerStore app loaded');
    
    // Check current page and load appropriate content
    if (document.getElementById('featuredProducts')) {
        loadFeaturedProducts();
    }
    
    if (document.getElementById('productsGrid')) {
        loadAllProducts();
        loadCategoriesForFilter();
    }
    
    // Update UI
    updateCartCount();
    updateLoginStatus();
    setupLogout();
    
    // Setup event listeners
    setupEventListeners();
    
    // Add animations
    addAnimations();
});

function setupEventListeners() {
    // Search form
    const searchForm = document.getElementById('searchForm');
    if (searchForm) {
        searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
            if (searchInput && searchInput.value.trim()) {
                window.location.href = `/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(searchInput.value.trim())}`;
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Mobile menu toggle (if needed)
    const mobileMenuBtn = document.getElementById('mobileMenuBtn');
    if (mobileMenuBtn) {
        mobileMenuBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleMobileMenu);
    }
}

async function loadFeaturedProducts() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/products');
        const products = await response.json();
        
        // Get featured products
        let featuredProducts = products.filter(p => p.isFeatured).slice(0, 6);
        if (featuredProducts.length === 0) {
            featuredProducts = products.slice(0, 6);
        }
        
        const container = document.getElementById('featuredProducts');
        if (container) {
            container.innerHTML = featuredProducts.map(product => `
                <div class="card">
                    ${product.isFeatured ? '<span class="card-badge">Featured</span>' : ''}
                    <img src="${product.image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593640408182-31c70c8268f5?w=400&h=300&fit=crop'}" 
                         alt="${product.name}" 
                         class="card-image">
                    <div class="card-content">
                        <h3>${product.name}</h3>
                        <p>${product.description ? product.description.substring(0, 80) + '...' : 'High-quality computer product'}</p>
                        <div class="price">$${product.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        <div class="stock-status">
                            ${product.quantity > 0 ? 
                                `<span class="in-stock"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> In Stock</span>` : 
                                '<span class="out-of-stock"><i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> Out of Stock</span>'
                            }
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-actions">
                            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" onclick="addToCart('${product._id}')" ${product.quantity <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                                <i class="fas fa-cart-plus"></i> Add to Cart
                            </button>
                            <a href="/product/${product._id}" class="btn btn-outline btn-block">
                                <i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View Details
                            </a>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading featured products:', error);
        const container = document.getElementById('featuredProducts');
        if (container) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div class="error-message" style="grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align: center; padding: 2rem; color: var(--danger);">
                    <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Failed to load products. Please try again later.
                </div>
            `;
        }
    }
}

async function loadAllProducts() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/products');
        const products = await response.json();
        
        const container = document.getElementById('productsGrid');
        const countElement = document.getElementById('productsCount');
        
        if (countElement) {
            countElement.textContent = `${products.length} products found`;
        }
        
        if (container) {
            if (products.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="no-products" style="grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align: center; padding: 4rem;">
                        <i class="fas fa-search" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--gray-light); margin-bottom: 1rem;"></i>
                        <h3 style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">No products found</h3>
                        <p style="color: var(--gray);">Try adjusting your filters or search terms</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            } else {
                container.innerHTML = products.map(product => `
                    <div class="card">
                        ${product.isFeatured ? '<span class="card-badge">Featured</span>' : ''}
                        <img src="${product.image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593640408182-31c70c8268f5?w=400&h=300&fit=crop'}" 
                             alt="${product.name}" 
                             class="card-image">
                        <div class="card-content">
                            <div style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">
                                <i class="fas fa-tag"></i> ${product.category?.name || 'Uncategorized'}
                            </div>
                            <h3>${product.name}</h3>
                            <p style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0.5rem 0;">
                                ${product.description ? product.description.substring(0, 60) + '...' : ''}
                            </p>
                            <div class="price">$${product.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                            <div class="stock-status">
                                ${product.quantity > 0 ? 
                                    `<span class="in-stock"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> In Stock (${product.quantity})</span>` : 
                                    '<span class="out-of-stock"><i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> Out of Stock</span>'
                                }
                            </div>
                            <div class="card-actions">
                                <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" onclick="addToCart('${product._id}')" ${product.quantity <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                                    <i class="fas fa-cart-plus"></i> Add to Cart
                                </button>
                                <a href="/product/${product._id}" class="btn btn-outline btn-block">
                                    <i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View Details
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading products:', error);
        showError('Failed to load products. Please try again later.');
    }
}

async function loadCategoriesForFilter() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/categories');
        const categories = await response.json();
        
        const filter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
        if (filter) {
            filter.innerHTML = '<option value="">All Categories</option>' + 
                categories.map(cat => `<option value="${cat._id}">${cat.name}</option>`).join('');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading categories:', error);
    }
}

// REAL CART FUNCTIONALITY
async function addToCart(productId, quantity = 1) {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart/add', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                productId: productId,
                quantity: quantity
            })
        });
        
        const data = await response.json();
        
        if (data.success) {
            showNotification('✓ Product added to cart!', 'success');
            updateCartCount();
        } else {
            showNotification(`✗ ${data.error || 'Failed to add to cart'}`, 'error');
        }
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error adding to cart:', error);
        showNotification('⚠ Network error. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

// Update cart count in navbar
async function updateCartCount() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
        const data = await response.json();
        
        const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
        if (cartCount) {
            const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
            cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
            cartCount.style.display = totalItems > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
    }
}

// Check login status and update UI
async function updateLoginStatus() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/me');
        const data = await response.json();
        
        const guestLinks = document.getElementById('guestLinks');
        const userLinks = document.getElementById('userLinks');
        const userName = document.getElementById('userName');
        
        if (data.success && data.user) {
            // User is logged in
            if (guestLinks) guestLinks.style.display = 'none';
            if (userLinks) {
                userLinks.style.display = 'flex';
                if (userName) {
                    // Get first name
                    const firstName = data.user.name.split(' ')[0];
                    userName.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-user"></i> ${firstName}`;
                    userName.href = '/profile';
                }
            }
            
            // Store in localStorage
            localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
            localStorage.setItem('isLoggedIn', 'true');
            
        } else {
            // User is not logged in
            if (guestLinks) guestLinks.style.display = 'flex';
            if (userLinks) userLinks.style.display = 'none';
            
            // Clear localStorage
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            localStorage.removeItem('isLoggedIn');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error checking login status:', error);
    }
}

// Setup logout functionality
function setupLogout() {
    const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('logoutBtn');
    if (logoutBtn) {
        logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', async function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to logout?')) {
                return;
            }
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/logout', {
                    method: 'POST'
                });
                
                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (data.success) {
                    // Clear localStorage
                    localStorage.removeItem('user');
                    localStorage.removeItem('isLoggedIn');
                    
                    // Show notification
                    showNotification('Logged out successfully', 'success');
                    
                    // Update UI and redirect
                    updateLoginStatus();
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = '/';
                    }, 1000);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Logout error:', error);
                showNotification('Logout failed. Please try again.', 'error');
            }
        });
    }
}

function applyFilters() {
    const category = document.getElementById('categoryFilter')?.value;
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('sortBy')?.value;
    
    console.log('Applying filters:', { category, sortBy });
    // Will implement filter logic later
}

function clearFilters() {
    const categoryFilter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('sortBy');
    
    if (categoryFilter) categoryFilter.value = '';
    if (sortBy) sortBy.value = 'newest';
    
    console.log('Filters cleared');
}

// Show notification function
function showNotification(message, type = 'info') {
    // Remove existing notifications
    const existing = document.querySelector('.notification');
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = `notification notification-${type}`;
    notification.innerHTML = `
        <div>${message}</div>
    `;
    
    document.body.appendChild(notification);
    
    // Remove after 3 seconds
    setTimeout(() => {
        notification.style.animation = 'slideOut 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (notification.parentNode) {
                notification.parentNode.removeChild(notification);
            }
        }, 300);
    }, 3000);
}

function addAnimations() {
    // Add scroll animations
    const observerOptions = {
        threshold: 0.1,
        rootMargin: '0px 0px -50px 0px'
    };
    
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                entry.target.classList.add('animate-in');
            }
        });
    }, observerOptions);
    
    // Observe cards
    document.querySelectorAll('.card, .mission-card, .category-card').forEach(card => {
        observer.observe(card);
    });
}

// Make functions available globally
window.addToCart = addToCart;
window.updateCartCount = updateCartCount;
window.showNotification = showNotification;
window.applyFilters = applyFilters;
window.clearFilters = clearFilters;

// public/js/auth.js
localStorage.setItem("token", data.token);

async function login(email, password) {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
        });
        return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Login error:', error);
        return { success: false, error: 'Network error' };
    }
}

async function register(userData) {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/register', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(userData)
        });
        return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Register error:', error);
        return { success: false, error: 'Network error' };
    }
}

async function logout() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' });
        return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Logout error:', error);
        return { success: false, error: 'Network error' };
    }
}




cart.js
// cart.js - Cart page functionality
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('Cart page loaded');
    loadCart();
    setupEventListeners();
});

let cartItems = [];

async function loadCart() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
        const data = await response.json();
        
        cartItems = data.cart || [];
        
        if (cartItems.length === 0) {
            showEmptyCart();
        } else {
            displayCart(cartItems);
        }
        
        updateCartCount();
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading cart:', error);
        showError('Failed to load cart. Please try again.');
    }
}

function displayCart(items) {
    const container = document.getElementById('cartContent');
    if (!container) return;
    
    // Calculate totals
    const subtotal = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
    const shipping = subtotal > 100 ? 0 : 9.99;
    const tax = subtotal * 0.08; // 8% tax
    const total = subtotal + shipping + tax;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="cart-container">
            <div class="cart-items">
                <h2>Cart Items (${items.length})</h2>
                ${items.map(item => `
                    <div class="cart-item" id="cart-item-${item.productId}">
                        <img src="${item.image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200?text=Computer'}" 
                             alt="${item.name}" 
                             class="cart-item-image">
                        
                        <div class="cart-item-details">
                            <h4>${item.name}</h4>
                            <div class="cart-item-category">${item.category || 'Uncategorized'}</div>
                            <div class="cart-item-price">$${item.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div class="quantity-controls">
                            <button class="quantity-btn" 
                                    onclick="updateQuantity('${item.productId}', ${item.quantity - 1})"
                                    ${item.quantity <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                                <i class="fas fa-minus"></i>
                            </button>
                            
                            <input type="number" 
                                   value="${item.quantity}" 
                                   min="1" 
                                   max="99"
                                   class="quantity-input"
                                   onchange="updateQuantity('${item.productId}', this.value)"
                                   id="qty-${item.productId}">
                            
                            <button class="quantity-btn" 
                                    onclick="updateQuantity('${item.productId}', ${item.quantity + 1})">
                                <i class="fas fa-plus"></i>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div class="item-total">
                            $${(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                        </div>
                        
                        <button class="remove-item" 
                                onclick="removeFromCart('${item.productId}')"
                                title="Remove item">
                            <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
                
                <div style="margin-top: 30px; text-align: right;">
                    <button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="clearCart()">
                        <i class="fas fa-trash-alt"></i> Clear Cart
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="cart-summary">
                <h2>Order Summary</h2>
                
                <div class="summary-row">
                    <span>Subtotal</span>
                    <span>$${subtotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
                </div>
                
                <div class="summary-row">
                    <span>Shipping</span>
                    <span>${shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : `$${shipping.toFixed(2)}`}</span>
                </div>
                
                <div class="summary-row">
                    <span>Tax (8%)</span>
                    <span>$${tax.toFixed(2)}</span>
                </div>
                
                <div class="summary-row total">
                    <span>Total</span>
                    <span>$${total.toFixed(2)}</span>
                </div>
                
                <button class="btn btn-primary checkout-btn" onclick="proceedToCheckout()">
                    <i class="fas fa-lock"></i> Proceed to Checkout
                </button>
                
                <a href="/products" class="continue-shopping">
                    <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Continue Shopping
                </a>
                
                <div class="secure-checkout">
                    <p><i class="fas fa-shield-alt"></i> Secure checkout. Your payment information is encrypted.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}

function showEmptyCart() {
    const container = document.getElementById('cartContent');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="cart-empty">
            <i class="fas fa-shopping-cart"></i>
            <h2>Your cart is empty</h2>
            <p>Looks like you haven't added any products to your cart yet.</p>
            <a href="/products" class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 20px;">
                <i class="fas fa-shopping-bag"></i> Start Shopping
            </a>
        </div>
    `;
}

function showError(message) {
    const container = document.getElementById('cartContent');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="cart-empty">
            <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
            <h2>Oops! Something went wrong</h2>
            <p>${message}</p>
            <button onclick="loadCart()" class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 20px;">
                <i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Try Again
            </button>
        </div>
    `;
}

function setupEventListeners() {
    // Update cart count in navbar
    updateCartCount();
}

async function updateQuantity(productId, newQuantity) {
    try {
        if (newQuantity < 1) {
            await removeFromCart(productId);
            return;
        }
        
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart/update', {
            method: 'PUT',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                productId: productId,
                quantity: parseInt(newQuantity)
            })
        });
        
        const data = await response.json();
        
        if (data.success) {
            // Update the input field
            const input = document.getElementById(`qty-${productId}`);
            if (input) {
                input.value = newQuantity;
            }
            
            // Reload cart display
            await loadCart();
            showNotification('✓ Cart updated!', 'success');
        } else {
            showNotification(`✗ ${data.error || 'Failed to update quantity'}`, 'error');
        }
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating quantity:', error);
        showNotification('⚠ Network error. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

async function removeFromCart(productId) {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to remove this item from your cart?')) {
        return;
    }
    
    try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/cart/remove/${productId}`, {
            method: 'DELETE'
        });
        
        const data = await response.json();
        
        if (data.success) {
            await loadCart();
            showNotification('✓ Item removed from cart', 'success');
        } else {
            showNotification(`✗ ${data.error || 'Failed to remove item'}`, 'error');
        }
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error removing from cart:', error);
        showNotification('⚠ Network error. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

async function clearCart() {
    if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to clear your entire cart?')) {
        return;
    }
    
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart/clear', {
            method: 'DELETE'
        });
        
        const data = await response.json();
        
        if (data.success) {
            await loadCart();
            showNotification('✓ Cart cleared successfully', 'success');
        } else {
            showNotification(`✗ ${data.error || 'Failed to clear cart'}`, 'error');
        }
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error clearing cart:', error);
        showNotification('⚠ Network error. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

function proceedToCheckout() {
    // Check if cart is empty
    if (cartItems.length === 0) {
        showNotification('Your cart is empty!', 'error');
        return;
    }
    
    // Redirect to checkout page
    window.location.href = '/checkout';
}

// Show notification function
function showNotification(message, type = 'info') {
    // Remove existing notifications
    const existing = document.querySelector('.notification');
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = `notification notification-${type}`;
    notification.innerHTML = `
        <div>${message}</div>
    `;
    
    document.body.appendChild(notification);
    
    // Remove after 3 seconds
    setTimeout(() => {
        notification.style.animation = 'slideOut 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => {
            if (notification.parentNode) {
                notification.parentNode.removeChild(notification);
            }
        }, 300);
    }, 3000);
}

// Update cart count in navbar
async function updateCartCount() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
        const data = await response.json();
        
        const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
        if (cartCount) {
            const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
            cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
            cartCount.style.display = totalItems > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
    }
}

// Make functions available globally
window.updateQuantity = updateQuantity;
window.removeFromCart = removeFromCart;
window.clearCart = clearCart;
window.proceedToCheckout = proceedToCheckout;
window.showNotification = showNotification;




product-detail.js
// product-detail.js - Product detail page functionality
console.log('Product detail script loaded');

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('DOM loaded');
    updateCartCount();
    
    // Extract product ID from URL
    const path = window.location.pathname;
    const pathParts = path.split('/');
    const productId = pathParts[2];
    
    console.log('Product ID:', productId);
    
    if (productId && productId.length > 10) {
        loadProductDetails(productId);
    } else {
        showError('Invalid product URL. Please check the link.');
    }
});

async function loadProductDetails(productId) {
    console.log('Loading product:', productId);
    
    try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/products/${productId}`);
        console.log('Response status:', response.status);
        
        if (!response.ok) {
            if (response.status === 404) {
                throw new Error('Product not found');
            }
            throw new Error(`Server error: ${response.status}`);
        }
        
        const product = await response.json();
        console.log('Product loaded:', product.name);
        
        displayProductDetails(product);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error:', error);
        showError(`Could not load product: ${error.message}`);
    }
}

function displayProductDetails(product) {
    const container = document.getElementById('productInfo');
    if (!container) {
        console.error('productInfo element not found!');
        return;
    }
    
    // Escape product ID for JavaScript
    const safeProductId = product._id.replace(/'/g, "\\'");
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="product-main">
            <div class="product-gallery">
                <img src="${product.image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593640408182-31c70c8268f5?w=600&h=400&fit=crop'}" 
                     alt="${product.name}">
            </div>
            
            <div class="product-info">
                <h1>${product.name}</h1>
                <div class="product-category">
                    <i class="fas fa-tag"></i> ${product.category?.name || 'Uncategorized'}
                </div>
                
                <div class="product-price">$${parseFloat(product.price || 0).toFixed(2)}</div>
                
                <div class="product-stock">
                    ${product.quantity > 0 ? 
                        `<i class="fas fa-check-circle" style="color: var(--success);"></i>
                         <span>In Stock (${product.quantity} available)</span>` : 
                        `<i class="fas fa-times-circle" style="color: var(--danger);"></i>
                         <span>Out of Stock</span>`
                    }
                </div>
                
                <div class="product-description">
                    <h3>Description</h3>
                    <p>${product.description || 'High-quality computer product with excellent performance. Perfect for gaming, work, or creative tasks.'}</p>
                </div>
                
                <div class="product-features">
                    <h3>Key Features</h3>
                    <ul>
                        <li>High-performance components for optimal speed</li>
                        <li>Reliable and durable construction</li>
                        <li>Great value for money</li>
                        <li>Perfect for gaming, work, or study</li>
                        <li>Easy to upgrade and maintain</li>
                        <li>Energy efficient design</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
                
                <div style="margin-top: 2rem; display: flex; gap: 1rem;">
                    <button onclick="addToCart('${safeProductId}')" 
                            class="btn btn-primary"
                            ${product.quantity <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}
                            style="padding: 1rem 2rem; font-size: 1.1rem;">
                        <i class="fas fa-cart-plus"></i> Add to Cart
                    </button>
                    
                    <a href="/products" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding: 1rem 2rem; font-size: 1.1rem;">
                        <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Products
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        
        <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 3rem;">
            <a href="/products" class="btn btn-primary">
                <i class="fas fa-shopping-bag"></i> Browse More Products
            </a>
        </div>
    `;
}

function showError(message) {
    const container = document.getElementById('productInfo');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div style="text-align: center; padding: 4rem; background: white; border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: var(--shadow);">
            <h2 style="color: var(--danger); margin-bottom: 1rem;">Oops!</h2>
            <p style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 2rem;">${message}</p>
            <div>
                <a href="/products" class="btn btn-primary">
                    <i class="fas fa-laptop"></i> Browse Products
                </a>
                <a href="/" class="btn btn-outline" style="margin-left: 1rem;">
                    <i class="fas fa-home"></i> Go Home
                </a>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}
