products.js
// products.js - Products page functionality
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('Products page loaded');
    loadCategories();
    setupFilterListeners();
    loadProducts();
    updateCartCount();
});

let currentProducts = [];

async function loadCategories() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/categories');
        const categories = await response.json();
        
        const categoryFilter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
        if (categoryFilter) {
            categoryFilter.innerHTML = `
                <option value="">All Categories</option>
                ${categories.map(cat => `<option value="${cat._id}">${cat.name}</option>`).join('')}
            `;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading categories:', error);
    }
}

async function loadProducts() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/products');
        currentProducts = await response.json();
        
        displayProducts(currentProducts);
        updateProductCount(currentProducts.length);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading products:', error);
        showError('Failed to load products. Please try again later.');
    }
}

function displayProducts(products) {
    const container = document.getElementById('productsGrid');
    if (!container) return;
    
    if (products.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="no-products" style="grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align: center; padding: 4rem; background: white; border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: var(--shadow);">
                <i class="fas fa-search" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--gray-light); margin-bottom: 1rem;"></i>
                <h3 style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">No products found</h3>
                <p style="color: var(--gray);">Try adjusting your filters or search terms</p>
                <button class="btn btn-outline mt-2" onclick="clearFilters()">
                    <i class="fas fa-times"></i> Clear All Filters
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }
    
    container.innerHTML = products.map(product => `
        <div class="card">
            ${product.isFeatured ? '<span class="card-badge">Featured</span>' : ''}
            <img src="${product.image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200?text=Computer'}" 
                 alt="${product.name}" 
                 class="card-image">
            <div class="card-content">
                <div style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">
                    <i class="fas fa-tag"></i> ${product.category?.name || 'Uncategorized'}
                </div>
                <h3>${product.name}</h3>
                <p style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0.5rem 0;">
                    ${product.description ? product.description.substring(0, 60) + '...' : ''}
                </p>
                <div class="price">$${product.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                <div class="stock-status">
                    ${product.quantity > 0 ? 
                        `<span class="in-stock"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> In Stock (${product.quantity})</span>` : 
                        '<span class="out-of-stock"><i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> Out of Stock</span>'
                    }
                </div>
                <div class="card-actions">
                    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" onclick="addToCart('${product._id}')" ${product.quantity <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                        <i class="fas fa-cart-plus"></i> Add to Cart
                    </button>
                    <a href="/product/${product._id}" class="btn btn-outline btn-block">
                        <i class="fas fa-eye"></i> View Details
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    `).join('');
}

function updateProductCount(count) {
    const countElement = document.getElementById('productsCount');
    if (countElement) {
        countElement.textContent = `${count} product${count !== 1 ? 's' : ''} found`;
    }
}

function setupFilterListeners() {
    const categoryFilter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('sortBy');
    
    if (categoryFilter) {
        categoryFilter.addEventListener('change', filterProducts);
    }
    
    if (sortBy) {
        sortBy.addEventListener('change', filterProducts);
    }
}

function filterProducts() {
    const category = document.getElementById('categoryFilter')?.value;
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('sortBy')?.value;
    
    let filtered = [...currentProducts];
    
    // Filter by category
    if (category) {
        filtered = filtered.filter(product => 
            product.category?._id === category || product.category === category
        );
    }
    
    // Sort products
    filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        switch (sortBy) {
            case 'price-low':
                return a.price - b.price;
            case 'price-high':
                return b.price - a.price;
            case 'name':
                return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
            default: // 'newest'
                return new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt);
        }
    });
    
    displayProducts(filtered);
    updateProductCount(filtered.length);
}

// Add to cart function
async function addToCart(productId, quantity = 1) {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart/add', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                productId: productId,
                quantity: quantity
            })
        });
        
        const data = await response.json();
        
        if (data.success) {
            showNotification('✓ Product added to cart!', 'success');
            updateCartCount();
        } else {
            showNotification(`✗ ${data.error || 'Failed to add to cart'}`, 'error');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error:', error);
        showNotification('⚠ Network error. Please try again.', 'error');
    }
}

function showError(message) {
    const container = document.getElementById('productsGrid');
    if (!container) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="error-message" style="grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align: center; padding: 4rem; background: white; border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: var(--shadow);">
            <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--danger); margin-bottom: 1rem;"></i>
            <h3 style="color: var(--danger); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Error Loading Products</h3>
            <p style="color: var(--gray); margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">${message}</p>
            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="loadProducts()">
                <i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Try Again
            </button>
        </div>
    `;
}

function showNotification(message, type = 'info') {
    // Create notification element
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = `notification notification-${type}`;
    notification.innerHTML = `
        <div>${message}</div>
    `;
    
    document.body.appendChild(notification);
    
    // Remove after 3 seconds
    setTimeout(() => {
        notification.style.animation = 'slideOut 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.body.removeChild(notification);
        }, 300);
    }, 3000);
}

// Update cart count
async function updateCartCount() {
    try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/cart');
        const data = await response.json();
        
        const cartCount = document.getElementById('cartCount');
        if (cartCount) {
            const totalItems = data.totalItems || 0;
            cartCount.textContent = totalItems;
            cartCount.style.display = totalItems > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error updating cart count:', error);
    }
}

// Make functions available globally
window.addToCart = addToCart;
window.filterProducts = filterProducts;
window.clearFilters = function() {
    const categoryFilter = document.getElementById('categoryFilter');
    const sortBy = document.getElementById('sortBy');
    
    if (categoryFilter) categoryFilter.value = '';
    if (sortBy) sortBy.value = 'newest';
    
    displayProducts(currentProducts);
    updateProductCount(currentProducts.length);
};




profile.js
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  if (!token) {
    window.location.href = "/login.html";
    return;
  }

  const res = await fetch("http://localhost:3000/api/users/profile", {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
    }
  });

  const user = await res.json();

  document.getElementById("profileName").innerText = user.name;
  document.getElementById("profileEmail").innerText = user.email;
  document.getElementById("profilePhone").innerText = user.phone;
});

scripts folder have:
seedData.js
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');
require('dotenv').config();

// Import all models
const User = require('./models/User');
const Category = require('./models/Category');
const Product = require('./models/Product');
const ProductLine = require('./models/ProductLine');
const Office = require('./models/Office');
const Employee = require('./models/Employee');
const Settings = require('./models/Settings');

const connectDB = require('./config/db');

async function seedData() {
    try {
        await connectDB();
        
        // Clear existing data (optional)
        // await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();
        
        // 1. Create default settings
        const settings = [
            {
                key: 'store_name',
                value: 'ComputerStore',
                type: 'string',
                category: 'general',
                label: 'Store Name',
                description: 'The name of your online store'
            },
            {
                key: 'currency',
                value: 'USD',
                type: 'string',
                category: 'general',
                label: 'Currency',
                description: 'Default currency for prices'
            },
            {
                key: 'tax_rate',
                value: 8.0,
                type: 'number',
                category: 'tax',
                label: 'Tax Rate',
                description: 'Sales tax rate in percentage'
            },
            {
                key: 'free_shipping_threshold',
                value: 100,
                type: 'number',
                category: 'shipping',
                label: 'Free Shipping Threshold',
                description: 'Minimum order amount for free shipping'
            },
            {
                key: 'shipping_cost',
                value: 9.99,
                type: 'number',
                category: 'shipping',
                label: 'Standard Shipping Cost',
                description: 'Standard shipping cost for orders below threshold'
            }
        ];
        
        await Settings.insertMany(settings);
        console.log('✓ Settings created');
        
        // 2. Create admin user
        const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash('admin123', 10);
        const adminUser = await User.create({
            name: 'Admin User',
            email: 'admin@computerstore.com',
            password: hashedPassword,
            role: 'admin',
            emailVerified: true
        });
        console.log('✓ Admin user created');
        
        // 3. Create office
        const office = await Office.create({
            officeCode: 'HQ001',
            name: 'Headquarters',
            address: {
                street: '123 Tech Street',
                city: 'San Francisco',
                state: 'CA',
                zipCode: '94107',
                country: 'USA'
            },
            phone: '+1 (555) 123-4567',
            email: 'hq@computerstore.com'
        });
        console.log('✓ Office created');
        
        // 4. Create employee
        const employeeUser = await User.create({
            name: 'John Manager',
            email: 'manager@computerstore.com',
            password: await bcrypt.hash('employee123', 10),
            role: 'employee',
            emailVerified: true
        });
        
        const employee = await Employee.create({
            user: employeeUser._id,
            employeeId: 'EMP001',
            department: 'management',
            position: 'Store Manager',
            office: office._id,
            salary: 60000
        });
        
        // Update office with manager
        office.manager = employee._id;
        await office.save();
        console.log('✓ Employee created');
        
        // 5. Create product lines
        const gamingLine = await ProductLine.create({
            productLine: 'GAMING',
            name: 'Gaming Series',
            description: 'High-performance gaming computers and accessories',
            category: '695b8805166fe4363a41eafa', // Gaming PCs category ID
            manufacturer: 'Alienware',
            warrantyPeriod: 24
        });
        
        const productivityLine = await ProductLine.create({
            productLine: 'PRODUCTIVITY',
            name: 'Productivity Series',
            description: 'Computers and accessories for work and productivity',
            category: '695b8805166fe4363a41eafb', // Laptops category ID
            manufacturer: 'Apple',
            warrantyPeriod: 12
        });
        console.log('✓ Product lines created');
        
        // 6. Update existing products with product lines and SKUs
        const products = await Product.find();
        
        for (const product of products) {
            // Assign product line based on category
            if (product.name.includes('Alienware') || product.name.includes('ROG')) {
                product.productLine = gamingLine._id;
                product.sku = `GAM-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
            } else if (product.name.includes('MacBook')) {
                product.productLine = productivityLine._id;
                product.sku = `PROD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
            } else {
                product.sku = `GEN-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
            }
            
            // Add more images
            product.images = [
                product.image,
                'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1499951360447-b19be8fe80f5?w=600&fit=crop',
                'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517077304055-6e89abbf09b0?w=600&fit=crop'
            ];
            product.mainImage = product.image;
            
            // Add specifications
            product.specifications = new Map([
                ['Processor', 'Intel Core i9'],
                ['RAM', '32GB DDR5'],
                ['Storage', '1TB NVMe SSD'],
                ['Graphics', 'NVIDIA RTX 4090'],
                ['Display', '27" 4K UHD']
            ]);
            
            // Add features
            product.features = [
                'High-performance components',
                'RGB lighting',
                'Advanced cooling system',
                'Upgradable design',
                'Premium build quality'
            ];
            
            // Add dimensions
            product.dimensions = {
                length: 50,
                width: 25,
                height: 45,
                weight: 15
            };
            
            // Add warranty
            product.warranty = {
                period: 24,
                type: 'Standard Warranty'
            };
            
            await product.save();
        }
        console.log('✓ Products updated with complete data');
        
        console.log('\n✅ Database seeding completed successfully!');
        console.log('\nDefault credentials:');
        console.log('Admin: admin@computerstore.com / admin123');
        console.log('Manager: manager@computerstore.com / employee123');
        
        process.exit(0);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Seeding error:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    }
}

seedData();





services folder have:
categoryService.js
const Category = require('../models/Category');

exports.getAll = async () => await Category.find();
exports.getById = async (id) => await Category.findById(id);
exports.create = async (data) => {
    const category = new Category({ name: data.name });
    return await category.save();
};
exports.update = async (id, data) => await Category.findByIdAndUpdate(id, data, { new: true });
exports.delete = async (id) => await Category.findByIdAndDelete(id);




productService.js
const Product = require('../models/Product');

exports.getAll = async () => await Product.find().populate('category');
exports.getById = async (id) => await Product.findById(id).populate('category');
exports.create = async (data) => {
    const product = new Product(data);
    return await product.save();
};
exports.update = async (id, data) => await Product.findByIdAndUpdate(id, data, { new: true });
exports.delete = async (id) => await Product.findByIdAndDelete(id);

seed.js
// seed.js - WINDOWS COMPATIBLE SEED SCRIPT
require('dotenv').config();
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');

// Connect to MongoDB
const mongoURI = process.env.MONGO_URI || 'mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017/computer_store';
console.log('🔗 Connecting to MongoDB...');

mongoose.connect(mongoURI)
    .then(() => {
        console.log('✅ Connected to MongoDB');
        seedDatabase();
    })
    .catch(err => {
        console.error('❌ MongoDB connection error:', err);
        process.exit(1);
    });

// Import models
const Category = require('./models/Category');
const Product = require('./models/Product');
const User = require('./models/User');

async function seedDatabase() {
    try {
        console.log('🗑️  Clearing existing data...');
        
        // Clear in order to avoid reference errors
        await Product.deleteMany({});
        await Category.deleteMany({});
        await User.deleteMany({});
        
        console.log('✅ Existing data cleared');
        
        // ========== CREATE CATEGORIES ==========
        console.log('📂 Creating categories...');
        const categories = [
            { 
                name: 'Gaming PCs', 
                description: 'High-performance gaming computers',
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            { 
                name: 'Laptops', 
                description: 'Portable computers and notebooks',
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            { 
                name: 'Components', 
                description: 'Computer parts and components',
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            { 
                name: 'Peripherals', 
                description: 'Keyboards, mice, and accessories',
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            { 
                name: 'Monitors', 
                description: 'Computer displays and screens',
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            { 
                name: 'Networking', 
                description: 'Routers, switches, and networking gear',
                createdAt: new Date()
            }
        ];
        
        const createdCategories = await Category.insertMany(categories);
        console.log(`✅ Created ${createdCategories.length} categories`);
        
        // Create a map for easy category reference
        const categoryMap = {};
        createdCategories.forEach(cat => {
            categoryMap[cat.name] = cat._id;
        });
        
        // ========== CREATE PRODUCTS ==========
        console.log('📦 Creating products...');
        const products = [
            {
                name: 'Alienware Aurora R15',
                description: 'High-end gaming desktop with RTX 4090 and i9 processor',
                price: 2999.99,
                quantity: 15,
                category: categoryMap['Gaming PCs'],
                image: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593640408182-31c70c8268f5?w=400&h=300&fit=crop',
                isFeatured: true,
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            {

            },

            },
            {

                isFeatured: false,
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            {
                name: 'RTX 4090 Graphics Card',
                description: 'NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 24GB',
                price: 1899.99,
                quantity: 12,
                category: categoryMap['Components'],
                image: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1593640408182-31c70c8268f5?w=400&h=300&fit=crop',
                isFeatured: false,
                createdAt: new Date()
            },
            {
                name: 'Mechanical Keyboard',
                description: 'RGB mechanical gaming keyboard with brown switches',
                price: 129.99,
                quantity: 50,
                category: categoryMap['Peripherals'],
                image: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1541140532154-b024d705b90a?w=400&h=300&fit=crop',
                isFeatured: false,
                createdAt: new Date()
            }
        ];
        
        const createdProducts = await Product.insertMany(products);
        console.log(`✅ Created ${createdProducts.length} products`);
        
        // ========== CREATE USERS ==========
        console.log('👤 Creating users...');
        
        // Create admin user
        const adminPassword = await bcrypt.hash('admin123', 10);
        await User.create({
            name: 'Admin User',
            email: 'admin@computerstore.com',
            password: adminPassword,
            address: '123 Tech Street, San Francisco, CA',
            phone: '+1 (555) 123-4567',
            createdAt: new Date()
        });
        
        // Create regular user
        const userPassword = await bcrypt.hash('user123', 10);
        await User.create({
            name: 'John Doe',
            email: 'john@example.com',
            password: userPassword,
            address: '456 User Avenue, New York, NY',
            phone: '+1 (555) 987-6543',
            createdAt: new Date()
        });
        
        console.log('✅ Created 2 users');
        
        // ========== FINAL SUMMARY ==========
        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(50));
        console.log('🎉 DATABASE SEEDING COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY!');
        console.log('='.repeat(50));
        console.log('\n📊 SUMMARY:');
        console.log(`• Categories: ${createdCategories.length}`);
        console.log(`• Products: ${createdProducts.length}`);
        console.log(`• Users: 2`);
        
        console.log('\n🔑 LOGIN CREDENTIALS:');
        console.log('Admin: admin@computerstore.com / admin123');
        console.log('User: john@example.com / user123');
        
        console.log('\n🚀 START SERVER:');
        console.log('npm start');
        
        console.log('\n🌐 TEST LINKS:');
        console.log('http://localhost:3000');
        console.log('http://localhost:3000/products');
        console.log('http://localhost:3000/api/products');
        
        // Exit successfully
        setTimeout(() => {
            console.log('\n✅ Seed script completed. You can now start the server.');
            process.exit(0);
        }, 1000);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('\n❌ ERROR SEEDING DATABASE:');
        console.error('Message:', error.message);
        
        if (error.errors) {
            console.error('\nValidation errors:');
            Object.keys(error.errors).forEach(key => {
                console.error(`• ${key}: ${error.errors[key].message}`);
            });
        }
        
        process.exit(1);
    }
}





server.js
// server.js - Clean Modular Version
require('dotenv').config();
const express = require('express');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
const cors = require('cors');
const bodyParser = require('body-parser');
const path = require('path');
const session = require('express-session');
const fs = require('fs');
const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');

// Import Models
const User = require('./models/User');
const Product = require('./models/Product');
const Category = require('./models/Category');

// Import Modular Routes (Created in Step 1 & 2)
const productRoutes = require('./routes/productRoutes');
const orderRoutes = require('./routes/orderRoutes');

const app = express();

// ==========================================
// 1. MIDDLEWARE
// ==========================================
app.use(cors());
app.use(bodyParser.json());
app.use(bodyParser.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
app.use(express.static('public')); // Serves CSS, JS, Images
app.use(session({
    secret: process.env.SESSION_SECRET || 'computer-store-secret',
    resave: false,
    saveUninitialized: true,
    cookie: { 
        secure: false, // Set to true if using HTTPS
        maxAge: 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 // 24 hours
    }
}));

// Initialize cart in session if it doesn't exist
app.use((req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.session.cart) {
        req.session.cart = [];
    }
    next();
});

// ==========================================
// 2. DATABASE CONNECTION
// ==========================================
mongoose.connect(process.env.MONGO_URI || 'mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017/computer_store')
    .then(() => console.log('✅ MongoDB Connected'))
    .catch(err => console.error('❌ MongoDB Error:', err));

// ==========================================
// 3. API ROUTES (MODULAR)
// ==========================================
app.use('/api/products', productRoutes);
app.use('/api/orders', orderRoutes);

// Helper API for Categories (Keep here for simplicity)
app.get('/api/categories', async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const categories = await Category.find();
        res.json(categories);
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message });
    }
});

// ==========================================
// 4. CART & AUTH API (To be modularized next)
// ==========================================

// --- Cart API ---
app.get('/api/cart', (req, res) => {
    res.json({
        success: true,
        cart: req.session.cart,
        totalItems: req.session.cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0),
        totalPrice: req.session.cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0)
    });
});

app.post('/api/cart/add', async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { productId, quantity = 1 } = req.body;
        const product = await Product.findById(productId);
        if (!product) return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Product not found' });

        const existingItemIndex = req.session.cart.findIndex(item => item.productId === productId);
        if (existingItemIndex > -1) {
            req.session.cart[existingItemIndex].quantity += parseInt(quantity);
        } else {
            req.session.cart.push({
                productId: product._id.toString(),
                name: product.name,
                price: product.price,
                image: product.image,
                quantity: parseInt(quantity),
                category: product.category?.name || 'Uncategorized'
            });
        }
        res.json({ success: true, cart: req.session.cart });
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to add to cart' });
    }
});

app.delete('/api/cart/clear', (req, res) => {
    req.session.cart = [];
    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Cart cleared' });
});

// --- Auth API ---
app.post('/api/auth/login', async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { email, password } = req.body;
        const user = await User.findOne({ email });
        if (!user || !(await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password))) {
            return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: 'Invalid credentials' });
        }
        req.session.userId = user._id;
        req.session.userName = user.name;
        res.json({ success: true, user: { name: user.name, email: user.email } });
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Login failed' });
    }
});

app.get('/api/auth/me', (req, res) => {
    if (req.session.userId) {
        res.json({ success: true, user: { name: req.session.userName } });
    } else {
        res.json({ success: false });
    }
});

app.post('/api/auth/logout', (req, res) => {
    req.session.destroy();
    res.json({ success: true });
});

// ==========================================
// 5. PAGE ROUTES (HTML SERVING)
// ==========================================
app.get('/', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html')));
app.get('/products', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'products.html')));
app.get('/product/:id', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'product-detail.html')));
app.get('/cart', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'cart.html')));
app.get('/checkout', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'checkout.html')));
app.get('/login', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'login.html')));
app.get('/register', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'register.html')));
app.get('/profile', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'profile.html')));
app.get('/about', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'about.html')));
app.get('/blog', (req, res) => res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'blog.html')));

// 404 Handler
app.use((req, res) => {
    res.status(404).sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', '404.html'));
});

// ==========================================
// 6. START SERVER
// ==========================================
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`🚀 Server running at http://localhost:${PORT}`);
});
